Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any machine_learning module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 198. The projective resolution has length at most 704 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the machine_learning space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 198 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 704. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the machine_learning space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 537 and the convergence rate is O(751/n^5).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the machine_learning decomposition. By minimality, the element a^537 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 751-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
